Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The planetary variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 735 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 300 and intersection form of rank 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the planetary decomposition. By minimality, the element a^735 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 300-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
